```
Perfect! Now let me create a comprehensive summary based on all the information I've gathered:

## Comprehensive Analysis: User Settings and Sandbox Card Display

### 1. User Model Settings/Preferences

**File:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/models/user.rb`

The User model stores settings as individual database columns (not a JSON store). Key settings fields:

- **Boolean settings:**
  - `admin` - Admin flag
  - `tailscale_auto_connect` - Auto-connect to Tailscale
  - `chrome_persist_profile` - Persist Chrome profile
  - `must_change_password` - Force password change on next login

- **String/Text settings:**
  - `smb_password` - SMB password (encrypted)
  - `ssh_public_key` - SSH public key
  - `terminal_emulator` - Terminal type (`"xterm"` or `"ghostty"`)
  - `tailscale_auth_key` - Tailscale authentication
  - `tailscale_state` - Tailscale status (`"disabled"`, `"pending"`, `"enabled"`)
  - `tailscale_network`, `tailscale_subnet`, `tailscale_container_id` - Tailscale config

- **Integer settings:**
  - `sandbox_archive_retention_days` - Archives retention (nullable, falls back to `Setting.instance.sandbox_archive_retention_days` or 30)

**User methods:**
- `tailscale_enabled?` - Check if Tailscale is enabled
- `tailscale_pending?` - Check if Tailscale setup is pending
- `tailscale_disabled?` - Check if Tailscale is disabled
- `effective_archive_retention_days` - Returns user's setting or global setting (line 63-67)

### 2. Global Settings Model

**File:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/models/setting.rb`

Singleton pattern (`Setting.instance`). Stores system-wide defaults:
- `sandbox_archive_retention_days` (default: 30)
- `default_vnc_enabled`, `default_mount_home`, `default_docker_enabled`
- `default_data_path`
- OAuth configs (GitHub, Google)
- SMTP settings

### 3. Dashboard Controller & Sandbox Card Display

**File:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/dashboard_controller.rb`

**`index` action (lines 2-7):**
- Loads all sandboxes with user and routes: `@sandboxes = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).order(:name)`
- Loads archived sandboxes: `@archived_sandboxes = Current.user.sandboxes.archived.includes(:routes).order(:name)`
- Computes active VNC IDs from filesystem: `@vnc_active_ids = running_vnc_ids`
- Fetches Tailscale IPs for each sandbox: `@tailscale_ips = tailscale_ips_for(@sandboxes)`

**`card` action (lines 9-18):**
- Updates individual sandbox card via Turbo Stream
- Renders partial: `"dashboard/sandbox"` with `sandbox`, `vnc_active`, `tailscale_ip` locals
- Handles missing sandboxes gracefully

### 4. Sandbox Card Rendering

**File:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/views/dashboard/_sandbox.html.erb` (18KB template)

**Data available per sandbox:**
```
Sandbox model fields:
- id, name, status (pending/running/stopped/destroyed/archived)
- container_id, image, image_version
- user_id, created_at, updated_at
- job_status, job_error, job_started_at (for tracking operations)
- mount_home, data_path (persistent data)
- tailscale (boolean), smb_enabled, temporary
- vnc_enabled, vnc_geometry, vnc_depth
- docker_enabled, persistent_volume
- ssh_port
- routes (association) - each has: domain, port, mode (http/tcp), public_port, url

Associated via user:
- user.name, user.smb_password, user.tailscale_*
- Local vars: vnc_active (boolean), tailscale_ip (string IP)
```

**Action Buttons on Sandbox Cards (lines 100-179):**

For **running** sandboxes:
- `terminal_sandbox_path(sandbox, type: "tmux")` - Open tmux terminal
- `terminal_sandbox_path(sandbox, type: "shell")` - Open shell terminal
- `vnc_sandbox_path(sandbox)` - Open VNC desktop (if `vnc_enabled?`)
- Snapshot button (opens modal, posts to `snapshot_sandbox_path`)
- `logs_sandbox_path(sandbox)` - View logs
- `stop_sandbox_path(sandbox)` - Stop sandbox

For **stopped** sandboxes:
- `start_sandbox_path(sandbox)` - Start sandbox
- `logs_sandbox_path(sandbox)` - View logs (if container exists)

For **failed jobs**:
- `retry_sandbox_path(sandbox)` - Retry operation

Always available:
- `sandbox_path(sandbox)` - Edit sandbox (delete link)
- "Destroy" button with confirmation dialog

**Pills/Feature Tags on Card (lines 49-97):**
- TEMP badge (if temporary)
- HOME badge - static or clickable SMB link if `mount_home?` and Tailscale IP available
- PERSISTED badge - static or clickable SMB link if data mounted and Tailscale IP available
- Tailscale badge (shows Tailscale IP if available)
- Route badges - clickable links to `route.url` (https://domain:port)

### 5. Sandbox Model Key Methods

**File:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/models/sandbox.rb`

- `job_in_progress?` - Returns true if `job_status` is present
- `job_failed?` - Returns true if `job_error` is present
- `start_job(status)` - Sets job_status, clears job_error
- `finish_job()` - Clears job fields
- `fail_job(error_message)` - Sets job_error, clears status

**Turbo Broadcasts:**
- `broadcast_prepend_to_dashboard` - New sandbox added (uses partial `dashboard/sandbox`)
- `broadcast_replace_to_dashboard` - Sandbox updated
- `broadcast_remove_from_dashboard` - Sandbox deleted/archived

### 6. Routes and Paths

**File:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/config/routes.rb`

Sandbox routes (lines 32-51):
```
POST   /sandboxes/:id/start          -> start_sandbox_path
POST   /sandboxes/:id/stop           -> stop_sandbox_path
POST   /sandboxes/:id/rebuild        -> rebuild_sandbox_path
POST   /sandboxes/:id/retry          -> retry_sandbox_path
DELETE /sandboxes/:id                -> sandbox_path (delete)
POST   /sandboxes/:id/terminal       -> terminal_sandbox_path
POST   /sandboxes/:id/vnc            -> vnc_sandbox_path
POST   /sandboxes/:id/snapshot       -> snapshot_sandbox_path
GET    /sandboxes/:id/logs           -> logs_sandbox_path
GET    /sandboxes/:id/card           -> card_sandbox_path (dashboard.card action)
GET    /sandboxes/:id/stats          -> stats_sandbox_path
GET    /sandboxes/:id/metrics        -> metrics_sandbox_path
POST   /sandboxes/:id/archive_restore -> archive_restore_sandbox_path
DELETE /sandboxes/:id/purge          -> purge_sandbox_path
```

### 7. Dashboard View Structure

**Files:**
- `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/views/dashboard/index.html.erb` - Main dashboard (subscribes to Turbo Stream)
- `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/views/dashboard/_sandbox.html.erb` - Active sandbox card
- `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/views/dashboard/_archived_sandbox.html.erb` - Archived sandbox card
- `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/views/dashboard/_sandbox_stats.html.erb` - Stats partial

The dashboard uses:
- Turbo Streams for real-time updates: `turbo_stream_from [Current.user, "dashboard"]`
- Stimulus controllers for polling: `sandbox-polling` and `sparkline`
- Modal for snapshot creation (inline per card)
```

```
153	                  onclick="(function(){var d=new Date();var ts='snap-'+d.getFullYear()+(String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'))+(String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0'))+'-'+(String(d.getHours()).padStart(2,'0'))+(String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0'))+(String(d.getSeconds()).padStart(2,'0'));document.getElementById('snap-name-<%= sandbox.id %>').value=ts;document.getElementById('snap-modal-<%= sandbox.id %>').classList.remove('hidden');document.getElementById('snap-name-<%= sandbox.id %>').select();})()"

182	
183	  <%# Error display %>
184	  <% if sandbox.job_failed? %>
185	    <div class="text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 p-3 rounded border border-red-200 animate-fadeIn">
186	      <div class="flex items-start gap-2">
187	        <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor" class="w-5 h-5 shrink-0 mt-0.5">
188	          <path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M18 10a8 8 0 1 1-16 0 8 8 0 0 1 16 0Zm-8-5a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v4.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-4.5A.75.75 0 0 1 10 5Zm0 10a1 1 0 1 0 0-2 1 1 0 0 0 0 2Z" clip-rule="evenodd" />
189	        </svg>
190	        <div>
191	          <strong class="font-medium">Operation Failed</strong>
192	          <p class="mt-1 text-xs"><%= sandbox.job_error %></p>
193	        </div>
194	      </div>
195	    </div>
196	  <% end %>
197	
198	  <%# Container info and stats %>
199	  <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 pt-1">
200	    <div class="text-xs text-gray-400">
201	      <% if sandbox.container_id.present? %>
202	        <span class="font-mono"><%= sandbox.container_id.first(12) %></span> ·
203	      <% end %>
204	      <span class="font-mono"><%= sandbox.image_version.present? ? sandbox.image_version : sandbox.image.sub("ghcr.io/thieso2/", "") %></span>
205	      · Created <%= time_ago_in_words(sandbox.created_at) %> ago
206	      <% if sandbox.status == "running" %>
207	        · Running for <%= time_ago_in_words(sandbox.updated_at) %>
208	      <% elsif sandbox.status == "stopped" %>
209	        · Stopped <%= time_ago_in_words(sandbox.updated_at) %> ago
210	      <% end %>
211	    </div>
212	
213	    <div class="shrink-0 text-right">
214	      <% if sandbox.status == "running" %>
215	        <div data-controller="sparkline"
216	             data-sparkline-metrics-url-value="<%= metrics_sandbox_path(sandbox, format: :json) %>"
217	             data-sparkline-stats-url-value="<%= stats_sandbox_path(sandbox, format: :json) %>"
218	             data-sparkline-compact-value="true">
219	          <div class="flex items-center gap-3 justify-end">
220	            <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
221	              <span class="text-xs text-gray-400">CPU</span>
222	              <span class="text-xs font-mono font-medium text-gray-500" data-sparkline-target="cpuValue">&mdash;</span>
223	              <div class="w-16" data-sparkline-target="cpuChart"></div>
224	            </div>
225	            <div class="flex items-center gap-1">
226	              <span class="text-xs text-gray-400">MEM</span>
227	              <span class="text-xs font-mono font-medium text-gray-500" data-sparkline-target="memValue">&mdash;</span>
228	              <div class="w-16" data-sparkline-target="memChart"></div>
229	            </div>
230	          </div>
231	        </div>
232	      <% end %>
233	    </div>
234	  </div>
235	</div>
236	
237	<%# Per-card snapshot modal %>
238	<div id="snap-modal-<%= sandbox.id %>" class="hidden fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 flex items-center justify-center z-50" onclick="if(event.target===this)this.classList.add('hidden')">
239	  <div class="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-xl w-full max-w-sm mx-4">
240	    <div class="px-5 py-4 border-b border-gray-200 flex items-center justify-between">
241	      <h3 class="text-base font-semibold text-gray-900">Snapshot — <%= sandbox.name %></h3>
242	      <button onclick="document.getElementById('snap-modal-<%= sandbox.id %>').classList.add('hidden')" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 text-lg leading-none">✕</button>
243	    </div>
244	    <%= form_with url: snapshot_sandbox_path(sandbox), method: :post, class: "px-5 py-4 space-y-3" do |f| %>
245	      <%= f.text_field :name,
246	            required: true,
247	            id: "snap-name-#{sandbox.id}",
248	            pattern: "[a-z][a-z0-9_\\-]*",
249	            class: "w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500 text-sm font-mono" %>
250	      <div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-3">
251	        <button type="button" onclick="document.getElementById('snap-modal-<%= sandbox.id %>').classList.add('hidden')"
252	                class="px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900">Cancel</button>
253	        <%= f.submit "Create Snapshot", class: "px-4 py-1.5 bg-blue-600 text-white text-sm font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 cursor-pointer" %>
254	      </div>
255	    <% end %>
256	  </div>
257	</div>
258
```

```
## Summary of Findings

I've located all the key components you requested in the Sandcastle Rails app:

### 1. User Settings/Profile Edit Page

**Controller:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/settings_controller.rb`

The SettingsController handles user settings with:
- **Line 7-14:** `update_profile` action that updates user profile with `profile_params`
- **Line 75-77:** `profile_params` method that permits these fields:
  - `email_address`
  - `full_name`
  - `github_username`
  - `chrome_persist_profile`
  - `sandbox_archive_retention_days`
  - `terminal_emulator` (ghostty or xterm)

**Also includes:**
- **Line 43-52:** `toggle_tailscale` action for toggling `tailscale_auto_connect` setting
- **Line 32-41:** `update_smb_password` action for setting SMB password

**View:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/views/settings/show.html.erb`

The settings page includes sections for:
- Profile (Line 18-55): Full name, email, GitHub username
- Terminal emulator selection (Line 153-170): Choose between Ghostty Web and xterm.js
- Tailscale (Line 83-132): Status display and auto-connect toggle
- VNC (Line 134-151): Chrome profile persistence
- SMB/File Sharing (Line 261-293): SMB password configuration

---

### 2. Sandbox Hostname Determination (SANDCASTLE_HOST)

**SANDCASTLE_HOST Usage Locations:**

1. **Guide View:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/views/pages/guide.html.erb:7`
   - Fetches: `host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "sandcastle.example.com")`
   - Used to display login and route setup instructions

2. **Terminal Manager:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/terminal_manager.rb:58`
   - Fetches: `host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")`
   - Used in Traefik configuration for terminal URL routing (Lines 62-68)

3. **VNC Manager:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/vnc_manager.rb`
   - Referenced for VNC hostname configuration

4. **Route Manager:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/route_manager.rb:185, 220`
   - Line 185: `host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")`
   - Line 220: `host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")`
   - Used for SSL certificate generation

5. **Production Config:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/config/environments/production.rb`
   - Action Cable URL, allowed hosts, and mailer config use SANDCASTLE_HOST

**SSH Connection Information:**

The app uses **Tailscale IPs for SSH connections** rather than traditional SSH ports. Users don't directly SSH to a hostname/port combination. Instead:

- Each sandbox gets a **Tailscale IP** when `tailscale: true` is set
- Users access the sandbox via the CLI using `sandcastle connect <name>` or `sandcastle ssh <name>`
- The Tailscale IP is shown on the sandbox detail page (displayed in dashboard pill - see `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/views/dashboard/_sandbox.html.erb:89`)
- For SMB file sharing, the connection uses the **Tailscale IP on port 445**: `smb://<tailscale-ip>/home` (Lines 144-150 of show.html.erb)

---

### 3. Users Controller - Update Action and Permitted Params

**API Users Controller:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/api/users_controller.rb`

- **Line 20-24:** `update` action calls `@user.update!(user_params)`
- **Line 41-43:** `user_params` method that permits:
  - `name`
  - `full_name`
  - `github_username`
  - `email_address`
  - `password`
  - `password_confirmation`
  - `ssh_public_key`
  - `admin`
  - `status`

**Admin Users Controller:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/admin/users_controller.rb`

- **Line 29-35:** `update` action for admin panel
- **Line 53-55:** `user_params` using `params.expect(user: [ ... ])` with same permitted fields as API controller

**User Model:** `/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/models/user.rb`

- Lines 43-57: Tailscale-related methods (`tailscale_enabled?`, `tailscale_pending?`, `tailscale_disabled?`, `tailscale_auto_connect?`)
- The model stores: `ssh_public_key`, `smb_password` (encrypted), `tailscale_auto_connect`, `terminal_emulator`, `sandbox_archive_retention_days`, etc.
```

```
1	class SettingsController < ApplicationController
2	  def show
3	    @user = Current.user
4	    @api_tokens = @user.api_tokens.active.order(created_at: :desc)
5	  end
6	
7	  def update_profile
8	    @user = Current.user
9	
10	    if @user.update(profile_params)
11	      redirect_to settings_path, notice: "Profile updated successfully."
12	    else
13	      render :show, status: :unprocessable_entity
14	    end
15	  end
16	
17	  def update_password
18	    @user = Current.user
19	
20	    unless @user.authenticate(params[:current_password])
21	      redirect_to settings_path, alert: "Current password is incorrect."
22	      return
23	    end
24	
25	    if @user.update(password_params)
26	      redirect_to settings_path, notice: "Password changed successfully."
27	    else
28	      redirect_to settings_path, alert: @user.errors.full_messages.join(", ")
29	    end
30	  end
31	
32	  def update_smb_password
33	    @user = Current.user
34	
35	    if @user.update(smb_password_params)
36	      SandboxManager.new.update_smb_password(user: @user)
37	      redirect_to settings_path, notice: "SMB password updated."
38	    else
39	      redirect_to settings_path, alert: @user.errors.full_messages.join(", ").presence || "Failed to update SMB password."
40	    end
41	  end
42	
43	  def toggle_tailscale
44	    @user = Current.user
45	
46	    if @user.update(tailscale_auto_connect: !@user.tailscale_auto_connect)
47	      status = @user.tailscale_auto_connect ? "enabled" : "disabled"
48	      redirect_to settings_path, notice: "Tailscale auto-connect #{status}."
49	    else
50	      redirect_to settings_path, alert: "Failed to update Tailscale settings."
51	    end
52	  end
53	
54	  def generate_token
55	    @user = Current.user
56	
57	    token_name = params[:name].presence || "Web UI Token"
58	    token, raw_token = ApiToken.generate_for(@user, name: token_name)
59	
60	    flash[:api_token] = raw_token
61	    redirect_to settings_path, notice: "API token '#{token_name}' generated. Make sure to copy it now - you won't be able to see it again!"
62	  end
63	
64	  def revoke_token
65	    @user = Current.user
66	    token = @user.api_tokens.find(params[:id])
67	    token_name = token.name
68	    token.destroy!
69	
70	    redirect_to settings_path, notice: "API token '#{token_name}' revoked."
71	  end
72	
73	  private
74	
75	  def profile_params
76	    params.require(:user).permit(:email_address, :full_name, :github_username, :chrome_persist_profile, :sandbox_archive_retention_days, :terminal_emulator)
77	  end
78	
79	  def password_params
80	    params.require(:user).permit(:password, :password_confirmation)
81	  end
82	
83	  def smb_password_params
84	    params.require(:user).permit(:smb_password)
85	  end
86	end
87	
```

```
1	class DashboardController < ApplicationController
2	  def index
3	    @sandboxes = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).order(:name)
4	    @archived_sandboxes = Current.user.sandboxes.archived.includes(:routes).order(:name)
5	    @vnc_active_ids = running_vnc_ids
6	    @tailscale_ips = tailscale_ips_for(@sandboxes)
7	  end
8	
9	  def card
10	    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).find(params[:id])
11	    authorize sandbox
12	    vnc_active = VncManager.new.active?(sandbox: sandbox)
13	    tailscale_ip = sandbox.tailscale? ? TailscaleManager.new.sandbox_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox) : nil
14	    render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(helpers.dom_id(sandbox), partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: sandbox, vnc_active: vnc_active, tailscale_ip: tailscale_ip })
15	  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
16	    # Sandbox was destroyed/archived while the dashboard was open — remove the card
17	    render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.remove("sandbox_#{params[:id]}")
18	  end
19	
20	  def stats
21	    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
22	    authorize sandbox, :stats?
23	
24	    if sandbox.status == "running" && sandbox.container_id.present?
25	      @stats = Rails.cache.fetch("sandbox_stats_full:#{sandbox.id}", expires_in: 4.seconds) do
26	        container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
27	        raw = container.stats(stream: false)
28	
29	        next nil if raw.blank?
30	
31	        networks = raw["networks"] || {}
32	        net_rx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["rx_bytes"] || 0 }
33	        net_tx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["tx_bytes"] || 0 }
34	
35	        blkio = raw.dig("blkio_stats", "io_service_bytes_recursive") || []
36	        disk_read = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "read" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
37	        disk_write = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "write" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
38	
39	        {
40	          cpu_percent: StatsCalculator.cpu_percent(raw),
41	          memory_mb: StatsCalculator.memory_mb(raw),
42	          memory_limit_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "limit") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
43	          net_rx: net_rx,
44	          net_tx: net_tx,
45	          disk_read: disk_read,
46	          disk_write: disk_write,
47	          pids: raw.dig("pids_stats", "current") || 0
48	        }
49	      end
50	    end
51	
52	    respond_to do |format|
53	      format.html { render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: @stats, sandbox: sandbox } }
54	      format.json { render json: @stats ? { cpu: @stats[:cpu_percent], mem: @stats[:memory_mb].round(1) } : { cpu: nil, mem: nil } }
55	    end
56	  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
57	    respond_to do |format|
58	      format.html { render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: nil } }
59	      format.json { render json: { cpu: nil, mem: nil } }
60	    end
61	  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
62	    respond_to do |format|
63	      format.html { render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: sandbox } }
64	      format.json { render json: { cpu: nil, mem: nil } }
65	    end
66	  end
67	
68	  private
69	
70	  def running_vnc_ids
71	    Dir.glob(File.join(VncManager::DYNAMIC_DIR, "vnc-*.yml")).filter_map { |f|
72	      File.basename(f).match(/\Avnc-(\d+)\.yml\z/)&.[](1)&.to_i
73	    }.to_set
74	  rescue
75	    Set.new
76	  end
77	
78	  def tailscale_ips_for(sandboxes)
79	    ts_sandboxes = sandboxes.select { |s| s.tailscale? && s.container_id.present? }
80	    return {} if ts_sandboxes.empty?
81	
82	    all_containers = Docker::Container.all
83	    container_map = all_containers.each_with_object({}) { |c, h| h[c.id] = c }
84	
85	    ts_sandboxes.each_with_object({}) do |sandbox, ips|
86	      container = container_map[sandbox.container_id]
87	      next unless container
88	      network = sandbox.user.tailscale_network
89	      next unless network.present?
90	      ip = container.info.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks", network, "IPAddress")
91	      ips[sandbox.id] = ip if ip.present?
92	    end
93	  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
94	    {}
95	  end
96	end
97
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/models/user.rb",
  "old_string": "  def effective_archive_retention_days\n    return sandbox_archive_retention_days if sandbox_archive_retention_days.present?\n\n    @effective_archive_retention_days_fallback ||= Setting.instance.sandbox_archive_retention_days || 30\n  end\nend",
  "new_string": "  def effective_archive_retention_days\n    return sandbox_archive_retention_days if sandbox_archive_retention_days.present?\n\n    @effective_archive_retention_days_fallback ||= Setting.instance.sandbox_archive_retention_days || 30\n  end\n\n  CUSTOM_LINK_SHOW_ON_VALUES = %w[all desktop tablet phone].freeze\n  CUSTOM_LINK_TEMPLATE_VARS = %w[user hostname ssh_port sandbox tailscale_ip].freeze\n\n  def expand_custom_link_url(url_template, sandbox:, tailscale_ip: nil)\n    hostname = ENV.fetch(\"SANDCASTLE_HOST\", \"localhost\")\n    url_template.gsub(/\\{(\\w+)\\}/) do |match|\n      case $1\n      when \"user\" then name\n      when \"hostname\" then hostname\n      when \"ssh_port\" then sandbox.ssh_port.to_s\n      when \"sandbox\" then sandbox.name\n      when \"tailscale_ip\" then tailscale_ip.to_s\n      else match\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  private\n\n  def validate_custom_links\n    return if custom_links.blank?\n    unless custom_links.is_a?(Array)\n      errors.add(:custom_links, \"must be an array\")\n      return\n    end\n    custom_links.each_with_index do |link, i|\n      unless link.is_a?(Hash) && link[\"name\"].present? && link[\"url\"].present?\n        errors.add(:custom_links, \"entry #{i + 1} must have a name and url\")\n      end\n      if link[\"show_on\"].present? && !CUSTOM_LINK_SHOW_ON_VALUES.include?(link[\"show_on\"])\n        errors.add(:custom_links, \"entry #{i + 1} show_on must be one of: #{CUSTOM_LINK_SHOW_ON_VALUES.join(', ')}\")\n      end\n    end\n  end\nend"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/settings_controller.rb",
  "old_string": "  def generate_token",
  "new_string": "  def update_custom_links\n    @user = Current.user\n    links = (params[:custom_links] || []).reject { |l| l[:name].blank? && l[:url].blank? }.map do |l|\n      { \"name\" => l[:name].to_s.strip, \"url\" => l[:url].to_s.strip, \"show_on\" => l[:show_on].presence || \"all\" }\n    end\n\n    if @user.update(custom_links: links)\n      redirect_to settings_path, notice: \"Custom links updated.\"\n    else\n      redirect_to settings_path, alert: @user.errors.full_messages.join(\", \")\n    end\n  end\n\n  def generate_token"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/javascript/controllers/custom_links_controller.js",
  "content": "import { Controller } from \"@hotwired/stimulus\"\n\nexport default class extends Controller {\n  static targets = [\"list\", \"template\"]\n\n  add(event) {\n    event.preventDefault()\n    const index = this.listTarget.children.length\n    const html = this.templateTarget.innerHTML.replace(/INDEX/g, index)\n    this.listTarget.insertAdjacentHTML(\"beforeend\", html)\n  }\n\n  remove(event) {\n    event.preventDefault()\n    event.target.closest(\"[data-custom-links-row]\").remove()\n  }\n}\n"
}
```

```
1	<% content_for(:title) { "Getting Started — Sandcastle" } %>
2	
3	<div class="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 py-8">
4	  <h1 class="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-2">Getting Started</h1>
5	  <p class="text-gray-500 mb-8">Everything you need to go from zero to a running sandbox.</p>
6	
7	  <% host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "sandcastle.example.com") %>
8	
9	  <%# ── Install ── %>
10	  <section class="mb-10">
11	    <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">1. Install the CLI</h2>
12	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
13	      Download the latest release from
14	      <%= link_to "GitHub Releases", "https://github.com/thieso2/Sandcastle/releases/latest", target: "_blank", class: "text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 underline" %>,
15	      then extract it:
16	    </p>
17	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code>tar xzf sandcastle_*.tar.gz
18	sudo mv sandcastle /usr/local/bin/</code></pre>
19	  </section>
20	
21	  <%# ── Login ── %>
22	  <section class="mb-10">
23	    <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">2. Log in</h2>
24	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">Point the CLI at this server and authenticate via your browser:</p>
25	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code>sandcastle login https://<%= host %></code></pre>
26	  </section>
27	
28	  <%# ── Tailscale ── %>
29	  <section class="mb-10">
30	    <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">3. Enable Tailscale <span class="text-sm font-normal text-gray-500">(recommended)</span></h2>
31	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
32	      Each sandbox gets its own Tailscale IP so you can reach services directly — no port forwarding needed.
33	    </p>
34	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code># Interactive login — opens a browser URL to authenticate
35	sandcastle tailscale enable
36	
37	# Or use an auth key for headless setups
38	sandcastle tailscale enable --auth-key tskey-auth-...</code></pre>
39	    <p class="text-gray-500 text-sm mt-2">After authenticating, approve the advertised subnet routes in the Tailscale admin console.</p>
40	  </section>
41	
42	  <%# ── Create ── %>
43	  <section class="mb-10">
44	    <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">4. Create a sandbox</h2>
45	
46	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3"><strong>Web UI</strong> — click the "Create Sandcastle" button on your dashboard to create a sandbox with all available options:</p>
47	    <ul class="list-disc list-inside text-gray-700 ml-4 space-y-1 text-sm">
48	      <li><strong>Name</strong> — unique identifier for your sandbox</li>
49	      <li><strong>Container Image</strong> — custom image or default</li>
50	      <li><strong>Snapshot</strong> — restore from an existing snapshot</li>
51	      <li><strong>Mount Home</strong> — persistent home directory across all sandboxes</li>
52	      <li><strong>Data Path</strong> — mount user data (or subpath) to /persisted</li>
53	      <li><strong>Tailscale</strong> — connect to your Tailscale network</li>
54	      <li><strong>Docker daemon</strong> — enable/disable Docker-in-Docker</li>
55	      <li><strong>Temporary</strong> — auto-remove when you disconnect</li>
56	    </ul>
57	
58	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3 mt-4"><strong>Or use the CLI</strong> for quick creation:</p>
59	
60	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3"><strong>Quick throwaway sandbox</strong> — deleted when you disconnect:</p>
61	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code>sandcastle create scratch --rm</code></pre>
62	
63	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3 mt-4"><strong>Persistent sandbox</strong> — keeps your home directory and data across restarts:</p>
64	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code>sandcastle create my-dev --home --data</code></pre>
65	
66	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3 mt-4"><strong>Lightweight sandbox</strong> — no Docker daemon or VNC (faster startup):</p>
67	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code>sandcastle create lean --no-docker --no-vnc</code></pre>
68	
69	    <div class="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg p-4 mt-4 text-sm text-blue-800">
70	      <strong>Tip:</strong> Make these flags the default with config or env vars:<br>
71	      <code class="bg-blue-100 px-1 rounded">sandcastle config set mount_home true</code><br>
72	      <code class="bg-blue-100 px-1 rounded">sandcastle config set data_path .</code><br>
73	      <code class="bg-blue-100 px-1 rounded">sandcastle config set vnc false</code><br>
74	      <code class="bg-blue-100 px-1 rounded">sandcastle config set docker false</code><br>
75	      <span class="text-blue-600 text-xs mt-1 block">Or via env: <code class="bg-blue-100 px-1 rounded">export SANDCASTLE_HOME=1 SANDCASTLE_DATA=. SANDCASTLE_VNC=false</code></span>
76	    </div>
77	  </section>
78	
79	  <%# ── Connect ── %>
80	  <section class="mb-10">
81	    <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">5. Connect</h2>
82	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">Two ways to get a shell:</p>
83	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code># tmux session (reconnectable, auto-starts stopped sandboxes)
84	sandcastle connect my-dev
85	
86	# Plain SSH (no tmux)
87	sandcastle ssh my-dev
88	
89	# Pass extra SSH options after --
90	sandcastle connect my-dev -- -L 8080:localhost:8080
91	sandcastle ssh my-dev -- -D 1080</code></pre>
92	
93	    <h3 class="text-base font-semibold text-gray-800 mt-4 mb-2">SSH agent forwarding</h3>
94	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
95	      The CLI automatically forwards your local SSH agent (<code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded">-A</code>)
96	      so you can use your SSH keys (e.g. for Git) inside the sandbox without copying them.
97	      This works with the default SSH protocol.
98	    </p>
99	
100	    <h3 class="text-base font-semibold text-gray-800 mt-4 mb-2">Mosh (persistent, roaming sessions)</h3>
101	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
102	      Use <a href="https://mosh.org" target="_blank" class="text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 underline">mosh</a>
103	      for a connection that survives network changes and high-latency links. Mosh is pre-installed in every sandbox.
104	    </p>
105	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code>sandcastle connect --mosh my-dev</code></pre>
106	    <div class="bg-yellow-50 border border-yellow-200 rounded-lg p-4 mt-3 text-sm text-yellow-800">
107	      <strong>Warning:</strong> Mosh does <strong>not</strong> support SSH agent forwarding.
108	      Mosh drops the SSH connection after bootstrap and switches to UDP, which has no agent forwarding support.
109	      Your <code class="bg-yellow-100 px-1 rounded">ssh-add -l</code> keys will not be available inside the sandbox.
110	      If you need agent forwarding (e.g. for Git over SSH), use the default SSH protocol instead.
111	    </div>
112	    <div class="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg p-4 mt-3 text-sm text-blue-800">
113	      <strong>Requirements:</strong> <code class="bg-blue-100 px-1 rounded">mosh</code> must be installed on your local machine
114	      (<code class="bg-blue-100 px-1 rounded">brew install mosh</code> on macOS,
115	      <code class="bg-blue-100 px-1 rounded">apt install mosh</code> on Debian/Ubuntu).
116	      Mosh uses UDP ports 60000–61000 — if your sandbox is reachable only via SSH tunneling, use
117	      <a href="#tailscale" class="underline">Tailscale</a> instead (it handles UDP natively).
118	    </div>
119	  </section>
120	
121	  <%# ── Web Terminal ── %>
122	  <section class="mb-10">
123	    <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">6. Web terminal</h2>
124	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
125	      Click <strong>tmux</strong> or <strong>shell</strong> on any running sandbox to open a browser-based terminal.
126	      No SSH client needed — just a web browser.
127	    </p>
128	    <div class="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg p-4 text-sm text-blue-800">
129	      <strong>How it works:</strong> ttyd runs inside each sandbox container, exposing two terminal endpoints:
130	      <strong>tmux</strong> (persistent, re-attachable session shared across browser tabs) and
131	      <strong>shell</strong> (a plain login shell).
132	      Both are protected by your existing login — no extra passwords required.
133	    </div>
134	  </section>
135	
136	  <%# ── VNC ── %>
137	  <section class="mb-10">
138	    <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">7. VNC</h2>
139	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
140	      Click the <strong>VNC</strong> button on any running sandbox to access Google Chrome with a graphical desktop environment.
141	      Perfect for browser automation, visual testing, or running GUI applications.
142	    </p>
143	    <div class="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg p-4 text-sm text-blue-800 mb-3">
144	      <strong>How it works:</strong> Each sandbox includes Google Chrome, Xvfb (virtual X server), and x11vnc.
145	      Clicking VNC launches a noVNC container that connects to the sandbox's VNC server, giving you a full graphical browser in your web browser.
146	    </div>
147	    <div class="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-lg p-4 text-sm text-green-800">
148	      <strong>Chrome profile persistence:</strong> Go to Settings → VNC to enable Chrome profile persistence.
149	      When enabled, your Chrome extensions, bookmarks, and settings are saved and persist across sandbox recreations.
150	      If you use <strong>Mount Home</strong>, profiles are stored in <code class="bg-green-100 px-1 rounded">~/.config/google-chrome</code> alongside all your other home directory files.
151	      Without Mount Home, profiles are stored separately in a dedicated directory.
152	    </div>
153	  </section>
154	
155	  <%# ── SMB File Sharing ── %>
156	  <section class="mb-10">
157	    <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">8. SMB file sharing</h2>
158	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
159	      Mount your sandbox home directory or <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded">/persisted</code>
160	      as a network drive from macOS, Windows, or Linux using SMB3.
161	      This is useful for editing files with your local IDE or dragging and dropping in Finder/Explorer.
162	    </p>
163	    <div class="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg p-4 text-sm text-blue-800 mb-3">
164	      <strong>Requirements:</strong> Tailscale must be enabled (for network access) and you must set an SMB password in
165	      <%= link_to "Settings → File Sharing", settings_path, class: "underline" %>.
166	    </div>
167	
168	    <h3 class="text-base font-semibold text-gray-800 mb-2">Setup</h3>
169	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code># Set your SMB password (stored encrypted on the server)
170	sandcastle smb set-password
171	
172	# Create a sandbox with SMB enabled
173	sandcastle create my-dev --smb --tailscale</code></pre>
174	
175	    <h3 class="text-base font-semibold text-gray-800 mt-4 mb-2">Connect</h3>
176	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code># macOS (Finder → Go → Connect to Server, or in Terminal):
177	open smb://&lt;tailscale-ip&gt;/home
178	open smb://&lt;tailscale-ip&gt;/persisted   # if --data was used
179	
180	# Windows (File Explorer → Map Network Drive):
181	\\&lt;tailscale-ip&gt;\home
182	
183	# Linux:
184	sudo mount -t cifs //&lt;tailscale-ip&gt;/home /mnt/sandbox \
185	  -o username=&lt;your-sandcastle-username&gt;,vers=3.0</code></pre>
186	    <p class="text-gray-500 text-sm mt-2">
187	      The Tailscale IP is shown on the sandbox detail page. Username is your Sandcastle username. Password is what you set with <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded">sandcastle smb set-password</code>.
188	    </p>
189	  </section>
190	
191	  <%# ── Manage ── %>
192	  <section class="mb-10">
193	    <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">9. Manage sandboxes</h2>
194	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code>sandcastle list              # Show all your sandboxes
195	sandcastle stop my-dev       # Stop a running sandbox
196	sandcastle start my-dev      # Start a stopped sandbox
197	sandcastle rebuild my-dev    # Recreate with latest image
198	sandcastle rename my-dev new # Rename a sandbox
199	sandcastle delete my-dev     # Archive sandbox (soft-delete, data kept)
200	
201	# Copy files to/from a sandbox
202	sandcastle cp file.txt my-dev:~/           # local → sandbox
203	sandcastle cp my-dev:~/data.csv .          # sandbox → local
204	sandcastle cp -r my-dev:~/project ./       # recursive</code></pre>
205	
206	    <h3 class="text-base font-semibold text-gray-800 mt-4 mb-2">Archive &amp; restore</h3>
207	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
208	      When you delete a sandbox it is <strong>archived</strong> rather than immediately purged.
209	      Archived sandboxes have no running container but their data is preserved on disk.
210	      After the retention period (default: 30 days) a background job permanently deletes them.
211	      Admins can adjust the system-wide retention on the Settings page, and each user can override it
212	      in their profile settings.
213	    </p>
214	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code># List archived sandboxes
215	sandcastle list --archived
216	
217	# Restore an archived sandbox (recreates and starts the container)
218	sandcastle unarchive my-dev
219	
220	# Permanently delete an archived sandbox immediately, bypassing the retention period
221	sandcastle purge my-dev</code></pre>
222	    <p class="text-gray-500 text-sm mt-2">
223	      Archived sandboxes also appear as a collapsible section on your dashboard with a <strong>Restore</strong> button.
224	      The sandbox name is freed immediately on archive, so you can create a new sandbox with the same name.
225	    </p>
226	  </section>
227	
228	  <%# ── Routes ── %>
229	  <section class="mb-10">
230	    <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">10. Routes</h2>
231	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
232	      Expose services running inside your sandbox to the outside world. Two modes are supported:
233	    </p>
234	
235	    <h3 class="text-base font-semibold text-gray-800 mb-2">HTTP routes (layer 7, custom domain + TLS)</h3>
236	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
237	      Route a custom domain to a port inside the sandbox. Traefik terminates TLS automatically.
238	    </p>
239	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code># Add an HTTP route — app.example.com → container port 8080 (default)
240	sandcastle route add myapp app.example.com
241	
242	# Add an HTTP route on a custom container port
243	sandcastle route add myapp api.example.com 3000</code></pre>
244	    <p class="text-gray-500 text-sm mt-2 mb-4">Point a DNS A/CNAME record at <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded"><%= host %></code> before adding the route. TLS certificates are provisioned automatically.</p>
245	
246	    <h3 class="text-base font-semibold text-gray-800 mb-2">TCP routes (layer 4, raw port forward)</h3>
247	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
248	      Forward a public TCP port (3000–3099) directly to a container port. Useful for databases,
249	      message brokers, or any non-HTTP service.
250	    </p>
251	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code># Forward a public port to container port 5432 (e.g. Postgres)
252	sandcastle route add myapp --tcp 5432
253	# Output: TCP port 3042 → container port 5432</code></pre>
254	    <p class="text-gray-500 text-sm mt-2 mb-4">A public port from the pool is assigned automatically. Connect with <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded"><%= host %>:&lt;public-port&gt;</code>.</p>
255	
256	    <h3 class="text-base font-semibold text-gray-800 mb-2">Managing routes</h3>
257	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code># List all routes for a sandbox (shows ID, mode, domain/port, container port)
258	sandcastle route list myapp
259	
260	# Remove an HTTP route by domain
261	sandcastle route delete myapp app.example.com
262	
263	# Remove any route by ID (use the ID from route list)
264	sandcastle route delete myapp --id 42</code></pre>
265	    <p class="text-gray-500 text-sm mt-2">Routes can also be managed from the sandbox detail page in the web UI.</p>
266	  </section>
267	
268	  <%# ── Snapshots ── %>
269	  <section class="mb-10">
270	    <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">11. Snapshots</h2>
271	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
272	      Save and restore the full state of a sandbox. Snapshots are composite — they capture
273	      the container filesystem (via <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded">docker commit</code>)
274	      and, when on a BTRFS filesystem, also the home directory and data directory.
275	    </p>
276	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code># Create a snapshot (all available layers)
277	sandcastle snapshot create my-dev before-migration
278	
279	# Create with a description and specific layers
280	sandcastle snapshot create my-dev checkpoint --label "before big refactor" --layers container,home
281	
282	# List all snapshots
283	sandcastle snapshot list
284	
285	# Show details of a specific snapshot
286	sandcastle snapshot show before-migration
287	
288	# Restore a snapshot into an existing sandbox (all layers)
289	sandcastle snapshot restore my-dev before-migration
290	
291	# Restore only the container layer
292	sandcastle snapshot restore my-dev before-migration --layers container
293	
294	# Create a new sandbox from a snapshot (all layers)
295	sandcastle create my-dev-v2 --from-snapshot before-migration
296	
297	# Clone only the container layer into a new sandbox
298	sandcastle create my-dev-v2 --from-snapshot before-migration --restore-layers container
299	
300	# Delete a snapshot
301	sandcastle snapshot delete before-migration</code></pre>
302	    <p class="text-gray-700 mt-3 mb-1">
303	      <strong>Web UI:</strong> Each sandbox has a detail page (click the sandbox name) with a Snapshots tab
304	      for creating, viewing, and deleting snapshots. The global
305	      <%= link_to "Snapshots page", snapshots_path, class: "text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 underline" %>
306	      shows all snapshots across all your sandboxes with clone-to-new-sandbox support.
307	    </p>
308	    <% unless (BtrfsHelper.btrfs? rescue false) %>
309	      <div class="bg-yellow-50 border border-yellow-200 rounded-lg p-4 text-sm text-yellow-800 mt-3">
310	        <strong>Note:</strong> BTRFS not detected on this host. Only the container layer is snapshotted.
311	        Home and data layers require BTRFS.
312	      </div>
313	    <% end %>
314	  </section>
315	
316	  <%# ── Settings ── %>
317	  <section class="mb-10">
318	    <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">12. User settings</h2>
319	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
320	      Click <strong>Settings</strong> in the navbar to manage your account preferences:
321	    </p>
322	    <ul class="list-disc list-inside text-gray-700 ml-4 space-y-1 text-sm">
323	      <li><strong>Profile</strong> — Update your email address and change your password</li>
324	      <li><strong>Tailscale</strong> — View connection status and toggle auto-connect for new sandboxes</li>
325	      <li><strong>Sandbox Archive</strong> — Set your personal archive retention period (overrides the system default)</li>
326	      <li><strong>VNC</strong> — Toggle Chrome profile persistence across sandbox recreations</li>
327	      <li><strong>API Tokens</strong> — Create, list, and revoke multiple named API tokens for different devices or applications</li>
328	    </ul>
329	    <div class="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg p-4 mt-4 text-sm text-blue-800">
330	      <strong>Using API tokens:</strong> Create tokens for different use cases (laptop, CI/CD, mobile).<br>
331	      <strong>Via Web UI:</strong> Go to Settings → click "New Token" → give it a name → copy the token<br>
332	      <strong>Via CLI:</strong> Run <code class="bg-blue-100 px-1 rounded">sandcastle token create laptop</code> to create a named token<br>
333	      <code class="bg-blue-100 px-1 rounded">sandcastle token list</code> to view all your tokens<br>
334	      <code class="bg-blue-100 px-1 rounded">sandcastle token revoke &lt;id&gt;</code> to revoke a specific token
335	    </div>
336	  </section>
337	
338	  <%# ── Inviting Users (Admin) ── %>
339	  <section class="mb-10">
340	    <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">13. Inviting users <span class="text-sm font-normal text-gray-500">(admin)</span></h2>
341	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
342	      Admins can invite new users via email. Each invite generates a unique, one-time registration link.
343	      New users click the link to choose a username and set a password — no pre-created account required.
344	    </p>
345	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3"><strong>To send an invite:</strong></p>
346	    <ol class="list-decimal list-inside text-gray-700 ml-4 space-y-1 text-sm mb-4">
347	      <li>Go to <strong>Admin → Invites</strong></li>
348	      <li>Click <strong>Invite User</strong></li>
349	      <li>Enter the invitee's email address and an optional personal message</li>
350	      <li>Click <strong>Send Invite</strong> — the invitee will receive an email with a registration link</li>
351	    </ol>
352	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3"><strong>Invite lifecycle:</strong></p>
353	    <ul class="list-disc list-inside text-gray-700 ml-4 space-y-1 text-sm mb-3">
354	      <li><strong>Pending</strong> — sent but not yet accepted; link is still valid</li>
355	      <li><strong>Accepted</strong> — user has registered; link is invalidated</li>
356	      <li><strong>Expired</strong> — link expired (default: 7 days) before being accepted</li>
357	    </ul>
358	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
359	      Pending invites can be revoked from the Invites admin panel at any time.
360	      Once revoked, the registration link immediately becomes invalid.
361	    </p>
362	  </section>
363	
364	  <%# ── Custom SSL Certificates ── %>
365	  <section class="mb-10">
366	    <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">14. Custom SSL certificates <span class="text-sm font-normal text-gray-500">(advanced)</span></h2>
367	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
368	      For internal networks, you can configure alternative hostnames with custom SSL certificates to avoid browser warnings.
369	    </p>
370	
371	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3"><strong>Generate certificates with mkcert:</strong></p>
372	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code># Install mkcert (macOS example)
373	brew install mkcert
374	mkcert -install
375	
376	# Generate certificate for your domains
377	mkcert sandcastle.internal sc.local</code></pre>
378	
379	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3 mt-4"><strong>Configure Sandcastle:</strong></p>
380	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">Add these variables to your <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">sandcastle.env</code> before installation:</p>
381	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code>SANDCASTLE_ALT_HOSTNAMES=sandcastle.internal,sc.local
382	REDACTED.internal+1.pem
383	REDACTED.internal+1-key.pem
384	SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE=selfsigned</code></pre>
385

393	  <%# ── CLI Preferences ── %>
394	  <section class="mb-10">
395	    <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">15. CLI preferences</h2>
396	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-3">
397	      Persist connection preferences in <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded">~/.sandcastle/config.yaml</code>.
398	      Environment variables override the file (useful with
399	      <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded">mise</code> or <code class="bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded">direnv</code>).
400	    </p>
401	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code># Use mosh instead of SSH by default
402	sandcastle config set connect_protocol mosh
403	
404	# Disable automatic tmux session attachment
405	sandcastle config set use_tmux false
406	
407	# Always mount persistent home directory on create
408	sandcastle config set mount_home true
409	
410	# Always mount user data directory on create
411	sandcastle config set data_path .
412	
413	# Disable VNC by default
414	sandcastle config set vnc false
415	
416	# Disable Docker (DinD) by default
417	sandcastle config set docker false
418	
419	# Show effective settings (merged from config file + env vars)
420	sandcastle config show</code></pre>
421	
422	    <p class="text-gray-700 mb-2 mt-4">Environment variable overrides (highest priority):</p>
423	    <pre class="bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 overflow-x-auto text-sm leading-relaxed"><code>SANDCASTLE_CONNECT_PROTOCOL=mosh  # "ssh" (default) | "mosh"
424	SANDCASTLE_USE_TMUX=false         # "true" (default) | "false"
425	SANDCASTLE_SSH_EXTRA_ARGS="-v"    # extra flags appended to ssh/mosh
426	SANDCASTLE_HOME=1                 # mount persistent home on create
427	SANDCASTLE_DATA=.                 # mount user data dir on create
428	SANDCASTLE_VNC=false              # disable VNC on create
429	SANDCASTLE_DOCKER=false           # disable Docker (DinD) on create</code></pre>
430
```

Now I understand the full picture. The `ssh_public_key` is a single text field. I'll add a `ssh_keys` jsonb column for structured multi-key management, keep backward compat, and update the entrypoint to handle multiple keys.

id: toolu_01UmQnMT1gf3moo4q4gxP5gp
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/db/migrate/20260416000002_add_ssh_keys_to_users.rb",
  "content": "class AddSshKeysToUsers < ActiveRecord::Migration[8.1]\n  def up\n    add_column :users, :ssh_keys, :jsonb, default: []\n\n    # Migrate existing ssh_public_key data into the new ssh_keys array\n    execute <<~SQL\n      UPDATE users\n      SET ssh_keys = jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('name', 'default', 'key', ssh_public_key))\n      WHERE ssh_public_key IS NOT NULL AND ssh_public_key != ''\n    SQL\n  end\n\n  def down\n    remove_column :users, :ssh_keys\n  end\nend\n"
}
```

```json
}
```

id: toolu_01L9NrqGGFKDuryYWJAegRNx
```
1	#!/bin/bash
2	set -e
3	
4	USERNAME="${SANDCASTLE_USER:-sandbox}"
5	SSH_KEY="${SANDCASTLE_SSH_KEY:-}"
6	
7	# Create user if not exists
8	if ! id "$USERNAME" &>/dev/null; then
9	    useradd -m -s /bin/bash -G sudo,docker "$USERNAME"
10	    echo "$USERNAME ALL=(ALL) NOPASSWD:ALL" >> /etc/sudoers.d/sandcastle
11	    chmod 0440 /etc/sudoers.d/sandcastle
12	fi
13	
14	# Set up SSH authorized keys (append if not already present, preserving
15	# any WeTTY keys that may have been injected for other sandboxes sharing
16	# this user's home directory via bind mount).
17	if [ -n "$SSH_KEY" ]; then
18	    SSH_DIR="/home/$USERNAME/.ssh"
19	    mkdir -p "$SSH_DIR" 2>/dev/null || true
20	    if [ -d "$SSH_DIR" ]; then
21	        if [ -f "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys" ]; then
22	            grep -qF "$SSH_KEY" "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys" || echo "$SSH_KEY" >> "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys"
23	        else
24	            echo "$SSH_KEY" > "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys"
25	        fi
26	        chmod 700 "$SSH_DIR" 2>/dev/null || true
27	        chmod 600 "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys" 2>/dev/null || true
28	    fi
29	fi
30	
31	# Set correct ownership and permissions on the home directory.
32	# chown -R covers .ssh, .local, and anything else created above.
33	# The host ensures 777 on the mount point before container start (via sudo
34	# chmod in SandboxManager#prepare_bind_mount), so the sandbox user can always
35	# write ~/.Xauthority, .ssh, etc.
36	#
37	# On BTRFS bind mounts with Sysbox ID-mapped mounts, chmod/chown may fail
38	# with EOVERFLOW ("Value too large for defined data type"). This is a known
39	# Sysbox bug (thieso2/sysbox#12). Log the error visibly but don't crash —
40	# the host has already set 777 on the mount point.
```

```json
  "new_string": "# Set up SSH authorized keys (append if not already present, preserving\n# any WeTTY keys that may have been injected for other sandboxes sharing\n# this user's home directory via bind mount).\n# SSH_KEY may contain multiple keys separated by newlines.\nif [ -n \"$SSH_KEY\" ]; then\n    SSH_DIR=\"/home/$USERNAME/.ssh\"\n    mkdir -p \"$SSH_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || true\n    if [ -d \"$SSH_DIR\" ]; then\n        touch \"$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys\"\n        while IFS= read -r key; do\n            [ -z \"$key\" ] && continue\n            grep -qF \"$key\" \"$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys\" || echo \"$key\" >> \"$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys\"\n        done <<< \"$SSH_KEY\"\n        chmod 700 \"$SSH_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || true\n        chmod 600 \"$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys\" 2>/dev/null || true\n    fi\nfi"
}
```

```
63	  end

67	
68	    @effective_archive_retention_days_fallback ||= Setting.instance.sandbox_archive_retention_days || 30
69	  end

73	    if keys.present?
74	      keys.join("\n")

80	  CUSTOM_LINK_SHOW_ON_VALUES = %w[all desktop tablet phone].freeze

82	
83	  def expand_custom_link_url(url_template, sandbox:, tailscale_ip: nil)
84	    hostname = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
85	    url_template.gsub(/\{(\w+)\}/) do |match|
86	      case $1
87	      when "user" then name
88	      when "hostname" then hostname
89	      when "ssh_port" then sandbox.ssh_port.to_s
90	      when "sandbox" then sandbox.name
91	      when "tailscale_ip" then tailscale_ip.to_s
92	      else match
93	      end
94	    end
95	  end
96	
97	  private
98	
99	  def validate_ssh_keys
100	    return if ssh_keys.blank?
101	    unless ssh_keys.is_a?(Array)
102	      errors.add(:ssh_keys, "must be an array")
103	      return
104	    end
105	    ssh_keys.each_with_index do |entry, i|
106	      unless entry.is_a?(Hash) && entry["key"].present?
107	        errors.add(:ssh_keys, "entry #{i + 1} must have a key")
108	        next
109	      end
110	      unless entry["key"].match?(/\Assh-\S+ \S+/)
111	        errors.add(:ssh_keys, "entry #{i + 1} does not look like a valid SSH public key")
112	      end
113	    end
114	  end
115	
116	  def validate_custom_links
117	    return if custom_links.blank?
118	    unless custom_links.is_a?(Array)
119	      errors.add(:custom_links, "must be an array")
120	      return
121	    end
122	    custom_links.each_with_index do |link, i|
123	      unless link.is_a?(Hash) && link["name"].present? && link["url"].present?
124	        errors.add(:custom_links, "entry #{i + 1} must have a name and url")
125	      end
126	      if link["show_on"].present? && !CUSTOM_LINK_SHOW_ON_VALUES.include?(link["show_on"])
127	        errors.add(:custom_links, "entry #{i + 1} show_on must be one of: #{CUSTOM_LINK_SHOW_ON_VALUES.join(', ')}")
128	      end
129	    end
130	  end
131	end
```

```
1	class SettingsController < ApplicationController
2	  def show
3	    @user = Current.user
4	    @api_tokens = @user.api_tokens.active.order(created_at: :desc)
5	  end
6	
7	  def update_profile
8	    @user = Current.user
9	
10	    if @user.update(profile_params)
11	      redirect_to settings_path, notice: "Profile updated successfully."
12	    else
13	      render :show, status: :unprocessable_entity
14	    end
15	  end
16	
17	  def update_password
18	    @user = Current.user
19	
20	    unless @user.authenticate(params[:current_password])
21	      redirect_to settings_path, alert: "Current password is incorrect."
22	      return
23	    end
24	
25	    if @user.update(password_params)
26	      redirect_to settings_path, notice: "Password changed successfully."
27	    else
28	      redirect_to settings_path, alert: @user.errors.full_messages.join(", ")
29	    end
30	  end
31	
32	  def update_smb_password
33	    @user = Current.user
34	
35	    if @user.update(smb_password_params)
36	      SandboxManager.new.update_smb_password(user: @user)
37	      redirect_to settings_path, notice: "SMB password updated."
38	    else
39	      redirect_to settings_path, alert: @user.errors.full_messages.join(", ").presence || "Failed to update SMB password."
40	    end
41	  end
42	
43	  def toggle_tailscale
44	    @user = Current.user
45	
46	    if @user.update(tailscale_auto_connect: !@user.tailscale_auto_connect)
47	      status = @user.tailscale_auto_connect ? "enabled" : "disabled"
48	      redirect_to settings_path, notice: "Tailscale auto-connect #{status}."
49	    else
50	      redirect_to settings_path, alert: "Failed to update Tailscale settings."
51	    end
52	  end
53	
54	  def update_custom_links
55	    @user = Current.user
56	    links = (params[:custom_links] || []).reject { |l| l[:name].blank? && l[:url].blank? }.map do |l|
57	      { "name" => l[:name].to_s.strip, "url" => l[:url].to_s.strip, "show_on" => l[:show_on].presence || "all" }
58	    end
59	
60	    if @user.update(custom_links: links)
61	      redirect_to settings_path, notice: "Custom links updated."
62	    else
63	      redirect_to settings_path, alert: @user.errors.full_messages.join(", ")
64	    end
65	  end
66	
67	  def update_ssh_keys
68	    @user = Current.user
69	    keys = (params[:ssh_keys] || []).reject { |k| k[:key].blank? }.map do |k|
70	      { "name" => k[:name].to_s.strip.presence || "key-#{SecureRandom.hex(3)}", "key" => k[:key].to_s.strip }
71	    end
72	
73	    if @user.update(ssh_keys: keys)
74	      redirect_to settings_path, notice: "SSH keys updated."
75	    else
76	      redirect_to settings_path, alert: @user.errors.full_messages.join(", ")
77	    end
78	  end
79	
80	  def generate_token
81	    @user = Current.user
82	
83	    token_name = params[:name].presence || "Web UI Token"
84	    token, raw_token = ApiToken.generate_for(@user, name: token_name)
85	
86	    flash[:api_token] = raw_token
87	    redirect_to settings_path, notice: "API token '#{token_name}' generated. Make sure to copy it now - you won't be able to see it again!"
88	  end
89	
90	  def revoke_token
91	    @user = Current.user
92	    token = @user.api_tokens.find(params[:id])
93	    token_name = token.name
94	    token.destroy!
95	
96	    redirect_to settings_path, notice: "API token '#{token_name}' revoked."
97	  end
98	
99	  private
100	
101	  def profile_params
102	    params.require(:user).permit(:email_address, :full_name, :github_username, :chrome_persist_profile, :sandbox_archive_retention_days, :terminal_emulator)
103	  end
104	
105	  def password_params
106	    params.require(:user).permit(:password, :password_confirmation)
107	  end
108

110	    params.require(:user).permit(:smb_password)
111	  end
112	end
```

> AGENT

Here's a summary of everything that was implemented:

## Custom Links Feature

**What it does:** Users can define custom URL links in Settings that appear as indigo pills on each running sandbox card in the dashboard. Each link has:
- **Name** - displayed label (e.g. "LaTerminal")
- **URL template** - with placeholder variables that get expanded per-sandbox
- **Show on** - device filter: `all`, `desktop`, `tablet`, or `phone` (uses CSS responsive breakpoints)

**Template variables available:**
| Variable | Expands to |
|---|---|
| `{user}` | Username |
| `{hostname}` | `SANDCASTLE_HOST` env var |
| `{ssh_port}` | Sandbox SSH port |
| `{sandbox}` | Sandbox name |
| `{tailscale_ip}` | Sandbox Tailscale IP |

**Example URLs:**
- `laterminal://open/{user}@{hostname}:{ssh_port}` - open in LaTerminal
- `ssh://{user}@{hostname}:{ssh_port}` - open SSH connection
- `ssh://{user}@{tailscale_ip}` - SSH via Tailscale IP

For tmux reconnection, apps like LaTerminal that support custom URL schemes can use the SSH port + hostname to connect and your tmux session will be waiting (since sandboxes auto-start tmux).

## SSH Keys Management

**What it does:** Replaces the "Coming soon" placeholder with a full management UI:
- Add/remove/edit multiple named SSH public keys
- Each key has a label (e.g. "MacBook", "iPad") and the public key itself
- Keys are stored as structured JSON in a new `ssh_keys` column
- Migration auto-imports any existing `ssh_public_key` data
- Entrypoint updated to handle multiple keys (one per line)
- Backward compatible: API `ssh_public_key` still works as fallback

## Files changed

- **2 migrations** - `custom_links` and `ssh_keys` jsonb columns on users
- **`app/models/user.rb`** - validation, `expand_custom_link_url()`, `all_ssh_keys_text()`
- **`app/controllers/settings_controller.rb`** - `update_custom_links` and `update_ssh_keys` actions
- **`config/routes.rb`** - 2 new PATCH routes
- **`app/views/settings/show.html.erb`** - Custom Links section + SSH Keys section
- **`app/views/dashboard/_sandbox.html.erb`** - renders custom link pills on sandbox cards
- **`images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh`** - handles multi-line SSH keys
- **2 Stimulus controllers** - `custom_links_controller.js`, `ssh_keys_controller.js`
- **`app/services/sandbox_manager.rb`** - uses `all_ssh_keys_text` for multi-key support
- **`app/views/pages/guide.html.erb`** - mentions new settings

Run `bin/rails db:migrate` (or `docker exec -it sandcastle-web ./bin/rails db:migrate` in dev) to apply. The sandbox image needs a rebuild for the multi-key entrypoint change to take effect on new containers.

> DEVELOPER

how can we make the ssh conenction ereconnect to the default tmux session be default?

> AGENT

The SSH protocol itself doesn't support passing a remote command via the `ssh://` URL scheme — it's just `ssh://[user@]host[:port]`. The command to run (`tmux attach` or `tmux new-session -A -s main`) is up to the terminal app.

However, there are a few approaches:

**1. Make tmux the default shell in the sandbox (simplest, works everywhere)**

The sandbox entrypoint could set the user's default shell (or `.bashrc`/`.profile`) to auto-attach tmux. That way *any* SSH connection — from any app, URL scheme, or CLI — automatically lands in the tmux session. No special URL parameters needed.

**2. App-specific URL schemes**

Some apps support command parameters:
- **LaTerminal**: `laterminal://open/{user}@{hostname}:{ssh_port}?command=tmux+new-session+-A+-s+main`
- **Prompt 3 / Blink**: similar custom scheme support
- **iTerm2**: `iterm2://ssh?host={hostname}&port={ssh_port}&user={user}&command=...`

But this is fragile and app-dependent.

**3. Use `.ssh/rc` or forced command server-side**

The sandbox could set up a default `ForceCommand` or `.ssh/rc` that wraps connections in tmux.

---

I'd recommend option 1 — it's the most universal. Want me to update the sandbox entrypoint to auto-attach tmux on SSH login? The standard pattern is adding this to the user's `.bashrc`:

```bash
if [[ -z "$TMUX" ]] && [[ "$SSH_CONNECTION" != "" ]]; then
    tmux new-session -A -s main
fi
```

This way every SSH connection (from any app, custom link, or CLI) reconnects the tmux session `main` automatically. The WeTTY tmux terminal already does `tmux new-session -A -s main`, so behavior would be consistent.

Shall I go with that approach?

> DEVELOPER

how about if i do not want a tmux session then?

> AGENT

Good point. If tmux is forced in `.bashrc`, there's no easy way to get a plain shell — which the dashboard already offers as a distinct option (tmux vs shell buttons).

Better approach: **make it a per-sandbox or per-user setting**, and use the custom links template system we just built to handle it. The actual connection behavior depends on the client app anyway.

Here are the clean options:

**Option A: Add a `{tmux_cmd}` template variable**

Custom links would look like:
- `laterminal://open/{user}@{hostname}:{ssh_port}?command={tmux_cmd}` 
- Where `{tmux_cmd}` expands to `tmux new-session -A -s main` (URL-encoded)

Users who don't want tmux just don't use `{tmux_cmd}` in their URL.

**Option B: Add a `{command}` variable with a user-configurable default**

A new user setting "default SSH command" (defaults to `tmux new-session -A -s main`, can be cleared for plain shell). Then `{command}` expands to whatever they set.

**Option C: Just document it — let users put the literal command in their URL template**

Users who want tmux write:
```
laterminal://open/{user}@{hostname}:{ssh_port}?command=tmux%20new-session%20-A%20-s%20main
```
Users who want a plain shell just omit the command parameter.

---

I'd lean toward **A** — add `{tmux_cmd}` as a template variable. It's zero-config, opt-in per link, and doesn't force anything. Want me to add that?

> DEVELOPER

yes

```
197	              <% end %>
198	            </select>
199	            <button type="button" data-action="custom-links#remove" class="text-red-500 hover:text-red-700 p-1" title="Remove">
```

> AGENT

Done. Two new template variables added:

- **`{tmux_cmd}`** — expands to `tmux new-session -A -s main` (plain text, for use in contexts that don't need encoding)
- **`{tmux_cmd_encoded}`** — expands to `tmux%20new-session%20-A%20-s%20main` (URL-encoded, for use in query parameters)

So a LaTerminal link with tmux reconnect would be:
```
laterminal://open/{user}@{hostname}:{ssh_port}?command={tmux_cmd_encoded}
```

And a plain SSH link without tmux is simply:
```
ssh://{user}@{hostname}:{ssh_port}
```

Users choose per-link whether they want tmux or not.

```
1	# Thin layer on top of the pre-built base image.
2	# Changes here (entrypoint, config, scripts) build in seconds.
3	# To rebuild the base: trigger the "Rebuild Sandbox Base Image" workflow
4	# or change files in images/sandbox/base/.
5	FROM ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox-base:latest
6	
7	ARG SANDCASTLE_VERSION=dev
8	
9	# Always pull latest security patches and package updates
10	RUN apt-get update && apt-get upgrade -y && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
11	
12	# Bake version into the image
13	RUN echo "${SANDCASTLE_VERSION}" > /etc/sandcastle-version
14	
15	# ssh-agent-switcher — daemon that proxies SSH agent requests to the most
16	# recent valid forwarded agent socket, surviving tmux detach/reattach.
17	ARG TARGETARCH
18	RUN SAS_VERSION="v1.0.1.1-tc" \
19	    && SAS_ARCH=$([ "$TARGETARCH" = "arm64" ] && echo "aarch64" || echo "x86_64") \
20	    && curl -fsSL "https://github.com/thieso2/ssh-agent-switcher/releases/download/${SAS_VERSION}/ssh-agent-switcher-${SAS_ARCH}-unknown-linux-gnu.tar.gz" \
21	       | tar -xz -C /usr/local/bin ssh-agent-switcher \
22	    && chmod +x /usr/local/bin/ssh-agent-switcher
23	
24	# SSH agent forwarding: prefer ssh-agent-switcher daemon socket, fall back
25	# to the stable symlink maintained by sc-tmux.
26	RUN printf '# ssh-agent-switcher daemon socket or symlink fallback\nif [ -S "/tmp/ssh-agent.${USER}" ]; then\n    export SSH_AUTH_SOCK="/tmp/ssh-agent.${USER}"\nelif [ -S "$HOME/.ssh/agent_sock" ]; then\n    export SSH_AUTH_SOCK="$HOME/.ssh/agent_sock"\nfi\n' \
27	       > /etc/profile.d/ssh-agent.sh
28	
29	COPY entrypoint.sh /entrypoint.sh
30	COPY docker-restart.sh /usr/local/bin/docker-restart
31	COPY sc-tmux.sh /usr/local/bin/sc-tmux
32	RUN chmod +x /entrypoint.sh /usr/local/bin/docker-restart /usr/local/bin/sc-tmux
33	
34	COPY startchrome.sh /usr/local/bin/google-chrome
35	RUN chmod +x /usr/local/bin/google-chrome \
36	    && ln -sf /usr/local/bin/google-chrome /usr/local/bin/startchrome
37	
38	COPY tmux.conf /etc/tmux.conf
39	COPY gitconfig /etc/gitconfig
40	
41	LABEL org.opencontainers.image.source="https://github.com/thieso2/sandcastle"
42	LABEL org.opencontainers.image.description="Sandcastle sandbox image — Ubuntu 25.10 with Docker-in-Docker, SSH, and dev tools"
43	LABEL org.opencontainers.image.licenses="MIT"
44	LABEL org.opencontainers.image.version="${SANDCASTLE_VERSION}"
45	
46	EXPOSE 22 5900 6080 7681 7682
47	EXPOSE 60000-61000/udp
48	
49	ENTRYPOINT ["/entrypoint.sh"]
50	
```
